G1901     D0.INTRO
          Section: D            Level: Household          CAI Reference: Q1901
          Type: Numeric         Width: 1                  Decimals: 0

          D0.
          IF Q765 IS (NE1)
                  Now a few questions about your family.
          ELSE Q506 IS (1)
                  Families and friends often help one another in different ways.
                  Part of our research involves finding out how they do that.
          IF the list of Q1892(1-20) IS (GT0)
                  The next questions are about your family and friends, beginning
                  with your children (and those of your (late)husband/wife/partner.)
          ELSE
                  The next questions are about your family and friends.
          END

          ELSE
                  Relatives can have important effects on your life, especially if
                  someone in your family needs help.  For this reason we'd like to
          IF the list of Q1892(1-20) IS (GT0)
                  ask some questions about your family and friends, beginning
                  with your children.  We'll be referring to them by first name
                  so that we can be sure we're talking about the correct person.

          ELSE
                  ask some questions about your family and friends.
          END

          END

          User note:  This preamble variable has been included in this data set in
          order to document questionnaire flow; all data values are blanks.
          ................................................................................
          13214     Blank. No data
